- **reported-count-reconciliation**: Use when a paper reports aggregate counts for a curated resource (e.g. total entries, novel additions, taxonomic coverage) and you must verify them directly from the released data files rather than trusting the text. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/reported-count-reconciliation))
- **reported-count-reproduction-from-deposited-data**: Use when a paper reports a database-overlap count and a significance test (e.g., 132 of 374 structures in LOTUS, chi-square 32.353) backed by a deposited dataset, and you must verify it independently. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/reported-count-reproduction-from-deposited-data))
- **reported-count-verification**: Use when a paper reports summary statistics (counts of items above thresholds) over a per-item results table — such as the Traitar Table S2 macroaccuracy table for 67 GIDEON phenotypes — and you must verify the reported numbers against the raw data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/reported-count-verification))
- **reported-count-verification-against-reference-values**: Use when a reported finding is a count of parameters grouped by sample type (EDTA plasma, serum, LiHep plasma) and delay type (pre- vs postcentrifugation) and you need to verify it against the PRIMA Panel stability time point data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/reported-count-verification-against-reference-values))
- **reported-figure-reproduction-verification**: Use when a reconstructed module must be validated by reproducing reported quantitative results, e.g. a claimed count of constructed meta-domains (3,334) or a coverage fraction (43.6%) derived from Pfam domain annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/reported-figure-reproduction-verification))
- **reported-fraction-verification**: Use when validating reported percentages of undetected known variants located in homopolymer runs or STRs, where undetected sets are defined by combinations of Illumina, PacBio, and ONT caller failures against a truth VCF. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/reported-fraction-verification))
- **reported-metric-verification**: Use when a paper reports aggregate counts (e.g., 5159 unique lipid ions, 1401 features, 26 lipid types) that must be verified by recounting them from the underlying per-feature annotation workbook in its supplementary files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/reported-metric-verification))
- **reported-metrics-recomputation-from-publication**: Use when a paper reports per-read execution time and energy per platform (e.g., HARU 0.94 ms/1.05 mJ vs Xeon 1.8 ms/358.3 mJ) and a derived metric such as EDP or speedup/energy ratios must be reproduced or verified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/reported-metrics-recomputation-from-publication))
- **reported-result-reproducibility-check**: Use when a source card reports specific numeric findings (e.g. alpha mean 0.633 ± SD 0.028 for 475 high-recombination genes) that must be independently reproduced from pre-calculated polymorphism/divergence tables using a named tool. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/reported-result-reproducibility-check))
- **reported-results-extraction-from-manuscript**: Use when a manuscript reports paired before/after optimization metrics across multiple biological studies (e.g. GSI, feature counts, annotations) and you need a machine-readable, arithmetic-consistency-checked results table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/reported-results-extraction-from-manuscript))
- **reported-results-reproduction-from-publication**: Use when reproducing published sequencing-depth and ASV/taxonomy counts from a 16S rRNA amplicon study — e.g., validating ~100,000 reads/sample, 1,568,227 ASVs per region, and 114 taxonomies from deposited FASTQ files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/reported-results-reproduction-from-publication))
- **reported-statistic-reproduction-verification**: Use when a paper reports specific correlation coefficients between community subnetwork properties (e.g., taxon richness of WGCNA subnetworks) and environmental time-series (e.g., chlorophyll a, sea surface temperature) and the task is to independently reproduce those exact statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/reported-statistic-reproduction-verification))
- **reported-table-count-reproduction**: Use when a paper reports exact per-method counts of significant features (e.g., significant metabolites at FWER alpha=0.05 in Table 3/Table S1) and you must verify those counts by re-executing the analysis from the released data and code. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/reported-table-count-reproduction))
- **reported-total-consistency-checking**: Use when a paper reports exact counts of workflow/method combinations (e.g., 5,904/48/1,968 NOREVA workflows under QCS, IS, and neither conditions) and you must reproduce them by re-enumerating the underlying combinatorial space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/reported-total-consistency-checking))
- **reported-transcript-count-reconciliation**: Use when reproducing or validating published IsoTools transcriptome reconstruction results from long-read BAM files and a GENCODE reference, and you must confirm gene/transcript counts match reported filtering-strategy totals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/reported-transcript-count-reconciliation))
- **reported-value-benchmark-verification**: Use when a published numeric result (e.g., a network statistic like a network density score reported in Fig. 1C of Kahraman et al., Scientific Reports 2020) must be independently reproduced from raw network data to validate a pipeline or database-derived value. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/reported-value-benchmark-verification))
- **reported-value-structured-record-encoding**: Use when a paper states a numeric performance or throughput figure tied to a specific instrument configuration (e.g., minutes per slide, magnification, workflow phase) that must be captured as a machine-readable record rather than free text. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/reported-value-structured-record-encoding))
- **reported-vs-computed-statistics-comparison**: Use when a paper reports a concrete quantitative outcome (cluster count, runtime, input size) and the underlying dataset or pipeline is re-accessible, so the reported figure can be independently recomputed and compared. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/reported-vs-computed-statistics-comparison))
- **reported-yield-extraction-from-methods-text**: Use when a paper's methods (or its Transparent Methods supplemental file) reports numeric detection/acquisition yields per slide condition, and those values must be extracted into a structured comparison table such as yield_comparison.csv. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/reported-yield-extraction-from-methods-text))

- **reposcape**: RepoScape graph/HUD and cognitive insights. Supports: update = refresh changed docs; update force = rescan selected docs. ([ThomasLix7/RepoScape](https://github.com/ThomasLix7/RepoScape/tree/HEAD/skills/reposcape) · ★3)
- **reposicao-caixa**: Decisão de COMPRA/reposição de fornecedor ponderada pelo IMPACTO NO CAIXA / capital de giro da Oben (distribuidora do grupo Colacor). Produz um MEMORANDO de decisão: comprar agora, segurar, parcelar, pagar à vista, negociar prazo, reduzir lote, antecipar (por aumento anunciado), comprar mais (promoção) ou priorizar por classe A/B/C. Use SEMPRE que o usuário estiver decidindo SE / QUANTO / QUANDO comprar de um fornecedor — mesmo sem dizer "memorando", e mesmo que o caixa/NCG entrem só como restrição da compra: "vale a pena comprar isso agora ou seguro", "quanto comprar do SKU X esse ciclo", "o… ([LucasSardenbergL/afiacao](https://github.com/LucasSardenbergL/afiacao/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reposicao-caixa))
- **reposicao-erario-servidor-tema-1009-stj**: Usar para pesquisar ou analisar: Reposição ao Erário por Erro Operacional (Tema 1.009 STJ + Súm. 249 TCU). Combinar com o protocolo LegalBR e conferir fontes oficiais antes de aplicar.
